Build a fresh image carrying the DeepGEMM host and MegaMoE header overlays from the previous commit, then run one ordinary Firmus PP4 serving job. The strict twelve-GPU gate decides after the Decode greedy gate whether the FC1 first-K kernel serves; the adopted kernel is prepared in every Prefill process before readiness and its dispatch lines are collected per worker.

The CPU proof removes 128 identity FMUL and 64 zero instructions at the FC1 entry and shortens the steady chunk from 1619 to 1611 instructions with unchanged registers and no spills, but the static code grows by about 15 percent, so only the serving score can decide adoption against the round 25 main configuration.
